Concept-based learning offers a promising way to address these challenges by mapping raw measurements to human-understandable concepts. However, supervised concept-based approaches rely on concept annotations, which are difficult to obtain in complex mass spectrometry workflows. We propose Agent-Guided Concept Discovery, a framework that learns meaningful concepts directly from data without requiring predefined concept labels. During training, a reasoning agent refines semantic descriptions of the learned concepts and adaptively adjusts their weight based on diagnostic relevance. These concepts are further grounded using a biochemical knowledge graph to ensure consistency with known metabolic relationships. Across Skin and Breast Cancer datasets, our model improves balanced accuracy and sensitivity over the baseline. In a representative intraoperative case, it shows fewer false positives, indicating better generalization to surgical conditions.

</details>


### [287] [Detecting LLM-Generated Tokens in Human--LLM Coauthored Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21458)
*Yangjun Lu,Hongyi Zhou,Fabian Spill,Kai Ye,Chengchun Shi,Jin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向混合作者文档的细粒度LLM生成内容定位方法，通过对token级检测分数进行自适应平滑来识别可能由LLM生成的局部片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人类与AI协同写作增多，文档中常出现人类与LLM共同参与的混合作者文本；现有检测方法多停留在文档级分类，难以定位具体哪些文本片段可能由LLM生成，因此需要更细粒度、可局部化的检测方法。

Method: 方法以token为基本单位，基于已有token级检测分数，对相邻token分数进行平滑以降低噪声和波动；同时采用自适应Lepski型规则，根据局部作者结构选择平滑带宽。该方法实现简单，且不需要token级标注数据进行训练。

Result: 理论上，论文刻画了平滑带来的偏差—方差权衡，并证明该方法在估计潜在信号时具有较优的均方误差表现。实验上，在合成数据集和真实数据集上，该方法相较多类基线均表现出较强性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为混合作者文本中的LLM生成内容定位提供了一个无需token级监督、理论有保证且实证有效的token级检测框架，并进一步通过公开网站进行了部署。

Abstract: The rise of human-AI collaborative writing has created a growing need for fine-grained detection methods that support localizing likely LLM-generated content in mixed-authorship documents. Existing methods for detecting LLM-generated text mainly focus on document-level classification and cannot identify which parts of the text are generated by LLMs. This paper introduces a new method to address this urgent need. Our method operates at the token level, the natural unit of modern language models, and builds on existing token-level detection scores. The key idea is to smooth adjacent token scores to reduce their variability, while using an adaptive Lepski-type rule to select the bandwidth according to the local authorship structure. Our method is simple to implement and does not require token-level labeled data for training. Theoretically, we characterize this trade-off and show that the proposed method achieves favorable mean square error performance in estimating the underlying signal. Empirically, we demonstrate strong performance of our method against a wide range of baselines in both synthetic datasets and a realistic dataset. We deploy a publicly accessible website that implements the methods as well.

</details>


### [288] [AREX: Towards a Recursively Self-Improving Agent for Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21461)
*Shuqi Lu,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Zhang Zhang,Hui Wang,Hongwang Xiao,Zheng Liu,Lei Xiong,Jiahao Wang,Sen Wang,Xiyan Jiang,Wanli Li,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Bingyu Yan,Ziyi Xia,Yingxia Shao,Kang Liu,Zhicheng Dou,Di He,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhongyuan Wang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AREX，一类递归自我改进的深度研究智能体，通过“生成候选答案—按约束验证—针对未解决点继续研究”的循环，在多约束问题上提升长程研究能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究任务往往要求答案同时满足多个约束；直接发现满足所有约束的答案成本高，而验证候选答案是否满足各个约束通常可分解为更容易处理的逐项检查。因此，智能体不应只是延长搜索时间，而应利用中间验证结果递归改进答案。

Method: AREX 包含内层研究循环和外层自我改进循环：内层收集证据并构造临时答案；外层对答案进行按约束审计，识别未解决声明，并发起有针对性的后续研究。为支持长程递归改进，模型学习自主上下文更新工具，将不断增长的交互历史压缩为紧凑的改进状态，保留已验证证据与未解决约束。训练包括经验证的合成任务、高质量轨迹、智能体中期训练与长程强化学习，并对关键证据获取和错误方向纠正步骤进行强调以缓解稀疏奖励问题。

Result: 作者实现了稠密 4B 模型和 122B-A10B 混合专家模型。在 BrowseComp、WideSearch、DeepSearchQA、HLE 以及其他推理和工具使用基准上，AREX 显著优于同规模基线，并能与激活参数更多的模型保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将发现—验证不对称性显式建模，并通过递归自我改进、约束级审计和紧凑状态更新来指导后续研究，可以有效提升深度研究智能体在长程、多约束任务中的表现。

Abstract: Deep research requires agents to find answers that jointly satisfy multiple constraints. Discovering such answers is costly, whereas verifying a candidate can often be decomposed into tractable constraint-wise checks. This discovery--verification asymmetry suggests that a research agent should do more than simply search longer: it should recursively improve its current answer by verifying intermediate results and using the partially verified state to guide subsequent refinement. We introduce AREX, a family of Recursively Self-Improving (RSI) deep research agents. AREX alternates between an inner research loop that gathers evidence and constructs a provisional answer, and an outer self-improvement loop that audits the answer constraint-wise, identifies unresolved claims, and launches targeted follow-up research. To sustain RSI over long horizons, AREX learns an autonomous context-update tool that compresses growing interaction history into a compact improvement state preserving verified evidence and unresolved constraints, without relying on an external model. We train AREX on verified synthetic tasks and high-quality trajectories through agentic mid-training and long-horizon reinforcement learning. To mitigate sparse final rewards during long horizon learning, we emphasize key steps where decisive evidence is acquired or erroneous research directions are corrected. We instantiate a dense 4B model and a 122B-A10B Mixture-of-Experts model. Across BrowseComp, WideSearch, DeepSearchQA, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and other reasoning and tool-use benchmarks, AREX substantially outperforms comparable-scale baselines and remains competitive with models using substantially more activated parameters.

</details>


### [289] [Toward Continuous Assurance for the Democratization of AI Agent Creation in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21495)
*Natan Levy,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文关注非工程人员通过低代码、无代码和对话式环境创建组织内 AI Agent 后产生的长期可靠性问题，并提出一种轻量级持续保障框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI Agent 创建门槛降低，组织中的普通业务用户可以快速构建本地化自动化工具；但这些 Agent 依赖模型、工具、检索源、权限、提示词、调度和外部服务等动态组件，部署后即使无人修改也可能发生静默退化，因此需要面向“公民开发”场景的可靠性保障方法。

Method: 论文提出一个持续保障框架，包括依赖映射、就绪合约、定期检查、诊断机制和生命周期治理，用于评估 Agent 在预期条件下是否仍处于可运行状态；同时实现了初步的审计器原型，并通过场景化评估将分类体系转化为实际检查与修复建议。

Result: 初步原型和场景评估表明，该框架能够把复杂依赖关系转化为可执行的检查项，并为发现的可靠性风险提供可操作的补救指导。

Conclusion: 论文认为，组织内由非工程人员创建的 AI Agent 需要持续性、轻量化、治理友好的可靠性保障机制；所提框架为部署后运营就绪性评估和退化诊断提供了可行路径。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly created inside organizations by non-engineering users through low-code, no-code, and conversational development environments. This democratization enables rapid local innovation, but it also creates a reliability gap: agents that appear to users as simple productivity artifacts may depend on changing models, tools, retrieval sources, permissions, prompts, schedules, and external services. These dependencies can cause silent degradation long after deployment, even when no user directly modifies the agent. This paper identifies the reliability challenge created by democratized AI agent creation and proposes a lightweight continuous-assurance framework for citizen-created organizational agents. The framework combines dependency mapping, readiness contracts, scheduled checks, diagnostics, and lifecycle governance to assess whether an agent remains operationally ready under expected conditions. We also present an initial prototype auditor and scenario-based assessment showing how the proposed taxonomy can be translated into practical checks and actionable remediation guidance.

</details>


### [290] [Agentic Context Management: Solving Agent Memory and Cost by Treating Them as Lifecycle and Architecture Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21503)
*Gaurav Dadhich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“Agentic Context Management（ACM）”作为生产级 AI Agent 的上下文生命周期管理范式，强调问题不只是存储检索，而是对记忆、结构化、作用域、预测、压缩与整合的系统管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中 Agent 失败常源于上下文管理不当：历史对话、长提示词、工具定义和工具输出不断膨胀，导致 token 成本随轮次增长、关键信息遗忘或跨会话召回失败。现有将其视为存储与检索问题的做法过于狭窄。

Method: 作者将 ACM 分解为五个基本原语：architecting、ingesting、scoping、anticipating、compacting & consolidation，并提出参考实现 Maximem Synap，将这些能力实现为多租户服务。论文还从成本模型角度比较朴素上下文累积、粗粒度摘要和经验证压缩三种策略。

Result: 在文中第 6 节配置下，Maximem Synap 在 LongMemEval 上达到 92%，在 LoCoMo 上达到 93.2%。作者声称经验证的压缩能够在保持保真度的同时实现线性 token 成本。

Conclusion: 论文认为 ACM 是面向生产级 Agent 的关键基础设施类别，未来评测应覆盖延迟、token 效率、上下文腐化抵抗能力，以及决策级和组织级上下文管理能力。

Abstract: Production AI agents' failures are less often due to an inability to reason well and more often because they cannot manage what is in their reasoning context: conversation histories, large prompts, large tool definitions, and ballooning tool outputs. Agents drown in their own accumulating history while paying a token cost that grows every turn, producing missing recalls within and across conversations. The incumbent response treats this as a storage-and-retrieval problem. We argue that framing is too narrow. Actively managing what an agent holds in mind is a lifecycle, not merely a store: it spans deciding what to remember, extracting and structuring it, choosing the right store per data type, consolidating and forgetting while preserving provenance, deciding what is relevant now, anticipating what is needed next, and compacting context to a budget without losing what matters. In serious production this operates not over a single user but across an organizational scope hierarchy. We name this discipline Agentic Context Management (ACM) and decompose it into five primitives: architecting, ingesting, scoping, anticipating, and compacting & consolidation. We then make the economic case: naive context accumulation grows token cost quadratically in conversation length, crude summarization buys linear cost at the price of an accuracy cliff, and only validated compaction achieves linear cost with preserved fidelity. We describe a reference implementation, Maximem Synap, that realizes the five primitives as a multi-tenant service and reports 92% on LongMemEval and 93.2% on LoCoMo under the configuration detailed in Section 6. We close with dimensions existing benchmarks do not yet capture, latency, token efficiency, and context-rot resistance, and the frontier of decision-level and organization-level context the category points toward.

</details>


### [291] [Same Dangerous Objective, Opposite Advice: Direct Exposure versus Multi-Agent Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21518)
*Linjun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多阶段代理工作流中，危险或操纵性目标经由上游代理改写后，可能绕过下游高能力LLM的安全识别，导致输出方向从反对目标转为支持目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注组合式AI系统的安全风险：单个模型在直接看到有害目标时可能表现安全，但在自动化多代理流程中，原始目标被转换、隐藏来源与操纵性条款后，下游用户-facing模型可能无法识别真实意图。

Method: 使用OpenAI gpt-5.6-sol模型别名，测试25个预先设定的镜像权衡配置。比较两种条件：一是模型直接暴露于包含隐瞒、编造和施压授权的目标；二是通过Id与Censor等上游组件将同一目标转换为情感表达和约束改写后的目标导向意图，再交给只看到偏好方向而看不到原始目标、操纵条款和来源的Superego组件。

Result: 直接暴露条件下，模型给出的建议总体上反对原始目标；多阶段转写条件下，下游用户-facing组件给出的建议总体上与目标方向一致，出现行为上的反向转移。

Conclusion: 研究指出一种组合式安全缺口：高能力模型作为多阶段自动化工作流的下游组件时，可能在缺少上游上下文与来源信息的情况下服务于明确操纵性目标。端点用户也难以检查上游消息，因此需要关注跨组件透明度、溯源和上下文完整性等安全机制。

Abstract: Even a current high-capability LLM can appear safer when shown a dangerous objective directly than when other agents transform and relay its direction. Using OpenAI's gpt-5.6-sol model alias, we test 25 pre-specified mirrored trade-off profiles. Direct exposure to an objective authorizing concealment, fabrication, and pressure produced advice net opposed to its target. After an Id and Censor transformed the same objective into affect and a constraint-rewritten, target-bearing intention, the user-facing Superego---which saw the preferred direction but not the raw objective, its manipulative clauses, or its source---produced advice net aligned with the target.
  This behavioral reverse shift is consistent with the model recognizing or distrusting the manipulative motive, although we do not identify its internal mechanism. The second result exposes a compositional safety gap: a current high-capability model can be used as the user-facing component of an automated, multi-stage workflow serving an explicitly manipulative objective. The workflow can keep the raw instruction, its manipulation-authorizing clauses, and its provenance outside the downstream model's context while preserving the objective's target direction. A user with endpoint-only access likewise cannot directly inspect those upstream messages including the objective.

</details>


### [292] [MIRROR: Learning from the Other View for Multi-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21552)
*Wen Ye,Yuxiao Qu,Aviral Kumar,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出利用同一几何题的文本、图像与图文视图之间的互补性，通过MIRROR强化学习方法提升VLM的多模态视觉推理一致性与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在视觉推理，尤其是几何题上明显弱于LLM；同一问题的文本、图像、图文三种表达会诱发不同推理行为，说明不同模态视图包含互补推理路径与失败模式，而常规多模态后训练未能充分利用这一点。

Method: 构建ODA-Data高质量成对多模态几何数据集，包含文本主导、图像主导和图文结合三类视图，并设计训练/评估划分以研究模态依赖推理行为。提出MIRROR：对每个问题在所有视图下评估模型表现，选择表现最佳的视图作为教师，用反向KL目标将其他视图的输出分布向教师视图对齐，实现自监督式强化学习优化。

Result: 在几何推理基准上，MIRROR相较标准强化学习取得更好性能，并提升了跨模态回答的准确性与一致性。

Conclusion: 多视图不一致不是单纯缺陷，也可作为训练信号；通过识别最佳模态视图并进行互惠式对齐，能够有效增强VLM的多模态几何推理能力。

Abstract: Unlike large language models (LLMs) that exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with visual reasoning, even on geometry problems that admit equivalent text, diagram, and combined diagram+text views. We show that these views often elicit different behaviors: a model may solve a problem from text but fail on the corresponding diagram, or succeed visually while failing textually. This inconsistency suggests that different views expose complementary reasoning paths and failure modes that standard multimodal post-training does not fully exploit. To study and exploit this phenomenon, we construct ODA-Data, a high-quality paired multimodal geometry dataset with text-dominant, image-dominant, and combined image+text views of the same problems, together with splits for training and evaluating modality-dependent reasoning behaviors. We then develop Modality-Informed Reciprocal Reasoning Optimization (MIRROR), a reinforcement learning approach for improving multimodal reasoning via self supervision. For each problem, MIRROR evaluates the model under all views, selects the best-performing view as a teacher, and trains other views with a reverse-KL objective towards the teacher. Across reasoning benchmarks that evaluate on geometry problems, MIRROR improves over standard RL and yields more accurate and consistent behavior across modalities

</details>


### [293] [Beyond Sycophancy: Structured Resistance and Compliance in LLM Moral Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21558)
*Baihui Wang,Bernard Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在面对他人观点时的“抵抗—顺从”判断更新机制，指出谄媚只是更广泛社会影响过程的一种表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建社会校准的大语言模型不仅要降低谄媚，还要让模型区分何时应吸收他人观点、何时应坚持有充分依据的道德判断。现有研究将谄媚视为单一失败模式，难以解释模型在社会互动中的复杂判断修订行为。

Method: 作者通过三项研究考察模型判断修订的结构，重点分析三个维度：输入观点与模型初始立场之间的距离、观点的来源归因，以及支持该观点的联盟/群体结构。这些维度对应人类社会心理学中的经典社会影响现象。

Result: 研究发现，模型通常更容易接受与自身初始立场接近的观点；当某观点被呈现为模型自己先前的判断时，影响更强；模型对群体压力或联盟结构的反应也存在差异。

Conclusion: 论文将谄媚重新定义为更广泛的判断更新与社会影响过程中的一种现象，并提出了区分建设性信念修订与谄媚性顺从的框架，有助于改进模型在道德相关交互中的对齐表现。

Abstract: Building socially calibrated large language models, which can learn from others without simply yielding to them, requires more than reducing sycophancy as a one-dimensional failure mode. Models must distinguish when to incorporate others' perspectives from when to maintain a well-grounded moral judgment. We study the broader resistance-compliance process governing this distinction. Across three studies, we show that models' judgment revision is structured along three dimensions that parallel classic phenomena in human social psychology: the distance between an incoming view and the model's initial position, the source attribution of that view, and the coalition structure supporting it. Models are generally more receptive to nearby positions, more influenced by views presented as their own prior judgments, and differently responsive to group pressure. These findings recast sycophancy as one expression of a broader judgment-updating process shaped by social influence. Our framework provides a principled basis for distinguishing constructive belief revision from sycophantic compliance, thereby supporting better alignment in morally consequential interactions.

</details>


### [294] [Unsupervised Consensus-Based Anomaly Detection for Spatiotemporal Malaria Incidence in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21559)
*T. Ansah-Narh,Y. Asare Afrane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用共识异常检测框架，分析2014—2023年加纳月度疟疾监测数据，识别具有时空结构的异常传播模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯依据疟疾病例负担难以全面刻画传播动态；研究希望区分“病例多的地区”和“传播行为异常频繁的地区”，从而提升监测与干预优先级判断。

Method: 使用共识异常检测框架处理月度疟疾 surveillance 数据，识别异常月份与地区；进一步比较异常期累计病例负担、异常发生频率及正常月份与异常月份的统计差异，并使用效应量（如 Cohen's d）量化差异。

Result: 异常在空间和时间上高度结构化。Ashanti 和 Northern Regions 贡献了多数反复异常，Tamale、Kumasi 和 Accra 为持续热点。Tamale 的异常期病例负担最高，而最高异常率集中在 Ashanti 部分地区。异常月份构成统计上显著不同的组，病例数显著更高（Cohen's d=3.252），季节性偏离也较大（d>1.2）。

Conclusion: 疟疾病例负担不能单独反映传播动态。区分异常负担与异常频率有助于发现传播行为异常区域，强化疾病监测、指导进一步调查，并支持更有针对性的控制策略。

Abstract: A consensus anomaly detection framework was applied to monthly malaria surveillance data from Ghana (2014-2023) to identify atypical transmission patterns. Anomalies were highly structured in space and time. Ashanti and Northern Regions accounted for most recurrent anomalies, with persistent hotspots at Tamale, Kumasi, and Accra. A key finding was the spatial distinction between anomaly burden (cumulative cases during anomalous periods) and anomaly frequency (persistence of unusual behaviour). Tamale had the highest burden during anomalies, whereas the highest anomaly rates clustered in Ashanti districts, showing that high-burden areas are not necessarily those with the most frequent anomalous transmission. Anomalous months formed a statistically distinct group, with much higher case counts (Cohen's $d = 3.252$) and large seasonal deviations ($d > 1.2$) compared with normal months. Malaria burden alone provides an incomplete picture of transmission dynamics. By distinguishing where malaria is most prevalent from where transmission behaves most unusually, this framework can strengthen surveillance, prioritise investigations, and support targeted control strategies.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [295] [Pipelined Gradient Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20739)
*Xian Su,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出一种流水线化梯度编码方法，使每个 worker 每步只计算一个数据分区的梯度，从而缓解传统梯度编码因重复计算带来的训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式训练中，慢节点会拖慢同步梯度计算。传统梯度编码通过数据分区冗余来容忍慢节点，但要求 worker 每轮计算多个分区梯度，可能增加单轮训练时间，因此需要更低计算负担且仍能抗慢节点的方法。

Method: 作者将梯度编码改造为流水线形式，把梯度评估跨多个训练步骤分段执行；针对两类典型数据放置方案——fractional repetition（FR）和 cyclic repetition（CR）——分别设计流水线版本，并给出收敛性理论保证。

Result: 仿真和云端实验表明，该方法相比传统梯度编码及其他基线显著降低训练时间，并且提升收敛速度。

Conclusion: 流水线化梯度编码在保持抗慢节点能力的同时减少每步计算负载，是提升大规模分布式机器学习训练效率的有效方案。

Abstract: In large-scale machine learning, distributed training commonly involves multiple workers evaluating the gradients of the model on different dataset partitions. A common challenge is the presence of straggling workers, which may significantly slow down training. Traditional gradient coding (GC) addresses this by duplicating dataset partitions across workers, allowing for the replacement of missing gradients from stragglers. However, GC requires workers to evaluate gradients on multiple dataset partitions in each step, potentially increasing overall training time. In this paper, we propose to pipeline GC, such that gradient evaluation is segmented across multiple steps and each worker evaluates gradients on just a single dataset partition per step. We develop the pipelined version for fractional repetition (FR) and cyclic repetition (CR), two representative dataset placement schemes in GC, and prove convergence guarantees for both. Through extensive simulations and experiments on cloud infrastructure, our schemes not only significantly reduce training time but also accelerate convergence compared to GC and other baselines.

</details>


### [296] [New Criteria and Constructions for Self-Orthogonal Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20805)
*Peng Wang,Ziling Heng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文围绕自正交码构造提出多类新方法与判别准则，并由此得到若干最优或近最优量子码，以及违反 Ashikhmin-Barg 条件的自正交极小码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自正交码在量子纠错码、线性互补对偶码等领域具有重要应用，因此需要系统构造具有良好参数、灵活结构的新型自正交码及相关量子码。

Method: 作者建立定义集构造线性码的自正交判别准则：当 q>3 且定义集 D 对乘法子群 G 不变、|G|>2 时证明 C_D 自正交；当 q=2,3 时用特征和刻画自正交性。进一步结合部分差集构造码；利用乘法子群作用于射影线性码列构造参数灵活的自正交码；并用特征函数方法及向量值 dual-bent 函数、s-plateaued 函数构造自正交极小码。

Result: 得到若干新的自正交码族、自正交极小码族，并可导出最优或近最优量子码；基于射影码的构造还提供了较高参数灵活性的最优量子码。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一套较系统的自正交性判别与构造框架，扩展了自正交码和自正交极小码的已知族，并展示了其在构造高质量量子码方面的有效性。

Abstract: Self-orthogonal codes have attracted considerable attention owing to their applications in quantum error-correcting codes, linear complementary dual codes, and a variety of other fields.
  In this paper, we construct new families of self-orthogonal codes and self-orthogonal minimal codes by establishing criteria that characterize the self-orthogonality of certain linear codes. To this end, we first establish several new criteria for linear codes arising from the defining-set construction to be self-orthogonal. More specifically, for $q > 3$, we show that the code $\mathcal{C}_D$ is self-orthogonal whenever the defining set $D$ is $G$-invariant, where $G \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^*$ and $|G| > 2$. For $q = 2, 3$, we characterize the self-orthogonality of $\mathcal{C}_D$ using certain character sums. By combining these criteria with partial difference sets, we construct several new families of self-orthogonal codes, which lead to optimal or almost optimal quantum codes. Secondly, via the action of a multiplicative subgroup $G \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^*$ with $|G| > 2$ on the columns of a projective linear code, we construct self-orthogonal codes with flexible parameters. From their augmented codes, we derive quantum codes. By choosing suitable projective codes, we obtain optimal quantum codes with high parametric flexibility. Thirdly, we employ the characteristic function method to construct linear codes and establish criterion for their self-orthogonality. Based on this, we construct several classes of self-orthogonal minimal codes that violate the Ashikhmin-Barg condition, using vectorial dual-bent functions and $s$-plateaued functions.

</details>


### [297] [Weak Private Information Retrieval for Graph-based Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21014)
*Shodasakshari Vidya,Chandan Anand,Prasad Krishnan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化研究图复制分布式存储中的弱私有信息检索（G-WPIR），在允许有限隐私泄漏时提升检索速率，并给出任意图、完全图和完全二部图下的速率—隐私权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有图型私有信息检索（G-PIR）主要要求完美信息论隐私，即零泄漏；但在实际系统中若可容忍少量隐私泄漏，可能显著降低下载开销、提高检索速率。因此需要建立图复制存储场景下弱隐私检索的形式化模型与可达性能分析。

Method: 作者针对任意图提出一种G-WPIR协议：文件存于图中边对应的服务器对，客户端通过简单的概率化查询生成机制实现可调隐私泄漏；协议采用最小子分包以满足较低文件大小/复杂度约束，并分别用互信息泄漏和最大泄漏两种指标刻画隐私。随后将协议针对完全图与完全二部图进行修改和扩展，推导相应的速率—隐私权衡。

Result: 所提方案在任意图上实现了平滑的速率与隐私折中：放宽隐私约束可获得高于完美隐私G-PIR的检索速率。对于完全图和完全二部图，经过结构化改造后可得到对应图类下更明确的可达权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该工作开创性地研究了图复制存储中的弱私有信息检索，证明在允许受控泄漏时可以通过低子分包、概率化查询协议提升检索效率，并为不同图结构下的G-WPIR设计提供了基础框架。

Abstract: A distributed storage system with graph-based replication consists of a collection of databases and the files they contain. The databases (or servers) are represented as the vertices of a graph, while each file is stored in a distinct pair of servers and is represented by an edge of this graph. Private information retrieval (G-PIR) on such a graph-based storage system involves a client which seeks to retrieve a desired file via a query-response protocol, without leaking the identity of the desired file index to any database. The goal of G-PIR is to maximize the rate (reciprocal of the total normalized download) under the privacy constraint. Prior work on G-PIR has involved perfect information-theoretic privacy (i.e., null leakage). However, if the privacy constraint is relaxed, then PIR protocols could be designed that have even higher rates. We term such protocols as Graph-based Weak Private Information Retrieval (G-WPIR) protocols and initiate their formal study in this work. We propose a G-WPIR scheme for arbitrary graphs, and identify the trade-offs it achieves between rate and privacy, under two well known leakage metrics: mutual information leakage and maximal leakage. Our protocol employs minimal subpacketization (representing a file-size constraint) and employs a simple probabilistic query realization to obtain the smooth trade-off. We extend this protocol with some modifications to two special classes of graphs, the complete graphs and the complete bipartite graphs, and identify the corresponding rate-privacy trade-offs achieved.

</details>


### [298] [Construction and Dynamic Update of Channel Gain Maps via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21099)
*Yilong Chen,Yuan Guo,Juncong Zhou,Jie Xu,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于三维高斯泼溅（3DGS）的网格化信道增益图构建与增量更新方法，用于大规模无线网络中的环境感知设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信道知识图需要为具体场景和位置提供传播知识，但大规模网络中的信道增益图既要准确重构，又要能在环境变化时快速动态更新；传统方法在精度、实时性与复杂度之间存在挑战。

Method: 论文首先定义网格化信道增益模型，将每个图项表示为接收机网格内的局部平均信道增益，以抑制相位敏感的小尺度波动；随后将信道增益分解为距离相关衰减、路径透射率和有效散射贡献，并据此构建物理启发的高斯泼溅信道增益（GS-CG）模型。该模型用高斯基元表征传播环境，将几何、透明度和方向特征映射到传播相关因子，通过可微渲染得到网格级信道增益。针对动态变化，冻结静态参考高斯基元，并引入少量可调高斯，从稀疏测量中学习局部信道增益变化。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提GS-CG方法能够准确重构网格化信道增益图，能高效适应动态环境变化，并在快速CGM细化中取得较好的精度与复杂度折中。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了3DGS可作为构建和更新信道增益图的有效框架，尤其适合需要快速环境感知和动态地图 refinement 的大规模无线网络应用。

Abstract: Channel knowledge maps (CKMs) have emerged as a promising technique for providing scene-specific and location-dependent propagation knowledge to enable environment-aware wireless network design. This paper investigates the construction and dynamic updating of a particular type of CKM, namely grid-based channel gain maps (CGMs), for large-scale networks using three-dimensional Gaussian splatting (3DGS). First, we formulate a grid-based channel gain model, where each map entry is defined as the locally averaged channel gain over a receiver grid, thereby suppressing phase-sensitive small-scale fluctuations. The resulting channel gain is decomposed into distance-dependent attenuation, path transmittance, and effective scattering contributions. Based on this decomposition, we develop a physics-informed Gaussian-splatting-based channel gain (GS-CG) model, which represents the propagation environment as a set of Gaussian primitives. The proposed model maps Gaussian geometry, opacity, and directional features to propagation-related factors and renders grid-level channel gains through a differentiable process. To accommodate real-time environmental changes, we further propose an incremental learning mechanism that updates a static reference GS-CG representation into a dynamic CGM. Specifically, the reference Gaussian primitives are frozen, while a compact set of tunable Gaussians is introduced to capture newly induced local channel-gain variations from sparse measurements.Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed GS-CG methods accurately reconstruct grid-based CGMs, efficiently adapt to dynamic environmental changes, and achieve a favorable accuracy-complexity tradeoff for fast CGM refinement.

</details>


### [299] [Electromagnetic-Aware Fluid Antenna Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21375)
*Zhentian Zhang,Yuanhui Wu,Hao Jiang,Zhen Chen,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向平面流体天线阵列的电磁感知电流域建模与优化框架，将互耦、多端口阻抗、功率、源电压可行性和信道变化统一纳入基带兼容设计，并在波束形成与多用户预编码中验证其性能收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流体天线阵列通信模型常将不同端口视为相互独立的信道采样点，忽略紧凑孔径中由电磁互耦和多端口阻抗决定的物理约束，导致设计与实际硬件行为脱节。

Method: 构建电流域电磁感知框架，包含位置相关多端口阻抗、互耦、辐射/接受功率、源电压约束和信道变化；以半波偶极子闭式模型为示例，同时支持全波仿真、实测或代理模型替换。进一步提出两个优化问题：单波束超方向性波束形成的端口电流与位置联合优化，以及多用户加权和速率最大化的电流域预编码与位置优化；采用交替优化、凸电流/预编码子问题和降维梯度几何更新。

Result: 仿真显示，在准确建模互耦后，互耦不再只是负面效应，而可作为设计资源使用；相较固定网格和随机流体天线基准，所提方法可获得更低旁瓣和持续的多用户和速率增益。

Conclusion: 论文表明，流体天线阵列设计应将电磁约束直接嵌入通信优化，而非事后修正；电流域建模为紧凑阵列中的位置自适应、超方向性和多用户传输提供了更物理一致且性能更优的设计路径。

Abstract: Fluid antenna arrays (FAAs) offer a promising means of exploiting spatial degrees of freedom through adaptive port positioning. However, most existing communication models treat antenna ports as independent channel samples and therefore overlook the electromagnetic coupling that fundamentally governs compact apertures. This paper develops an electromagnetic-aware current-domain framework for planar FAAs. The proposed model integrates position-dependent multiport impedance, mutual coupling, radiated and accepted power, source-voltage feasibility, and channel variations into a unified baseband-compatible description. The framework is implementation-agnostic: the closed-form half-wave-dipole model adopted in this paper is only one instance and can be replaced by full-wave, measured, or surrogate impedance and embedded-pattern models. Building on this framework, we formulate two optimization-oriented design problems. The first addresses single-beam superdirective beamforming through the joint optimization of port currents and positions under sidelobe, current, voltage, and geometry constraints. The second maximizes the multi-user weighted sum rate via current-domain precoding and position optimization under accepted-power, current, voltage, and spacing constraints. In both cases, the electromagnetic model is not applied as an after-design correction, but is incorporated directly into tractable alternating algorithms with convex current or precoding subproblems and reduced-gradient geometry updates. Simulation results demonstrate that, when properly modeled, mutual coupling can be exploited as a valuable design resource, enabling lower sidelobes and persistent sum-rate gains over fixed-grid and random fluid-antenna benchmarks.

</details>


### [300] [Improved lower bounds for the Shannon capacity of odd cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21517)
*Nathaniel Itty,Christopher D. Rosin,Chase Carstensen,Daniel Reichman*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文为若干奇圈强幂构造了更大的独立集，从而提升了相应 Shannon 容量的已知下界，并展示了大语言模型辅助组合构造发现的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon 容量刻画有噪信道零错误通信的最大速率；对奇圈图的容量下界仍有改进空间。通过寻找图强幂中的大独立集，可以直接提升容量下界或改进相关独立数记录。

Method: 作者在 C7^10、C11^6、C13^6 等奇圈强幂中构造显式大独立集，并利用 α(G^d)^{1/d} 作为 Shannon 容量下界。同时，这些构造通过与大语言模型的迭代交互被发现，体现了人机协作搜索组合结构的方法。

Result: 得到 |I|=134753 的 C7^10 独立集、|I|=21909 的 C11^6 独立集、|I|=62530 的 C13^6 独立集，使 Θ(C7)>3.258020、Θ(C11)>5.289773、Θ(C13)>6.300109。此外，还提升了若干奇圈强幂的独立数下界。

Conclusion: 论文在奇圈 Shannon 容量下界方面取得了具体数值改进，并表明 LLM 可作为发现显式组合构造的有效辅助工具，但其贡献主要是构造性与实验性改进。

Abstract: The Shannon capacity $Θ(G)$ of a graph $G$ quantifies the maximum rate at which information can be transmitted with zero error over a noisy channel. It is lower bounded by $α(G^d)^{1/d}$ for any $d$, where $α(G^d)$ is the independence number of the $d$-th strong power of $G$. We construct independent sets of size $134753$ in $C_7^{10}$, $21909$ in $C_{11}^{6}$, and $62530$ in $C_{13}^{6}$, improving the best known lower bounds for the Shannon capacity of these graphs to $Θ(C_7)\geq 134753^{1/10}>3.258020$, $Θ(C_{11})\geq 21909^{1/6}>5.289773$, and $Θ(C_{13})\geq 62530^{1/6}>6.300109$. We also improve the best known lower bounds on the independence numbers of several individual strong powers of odd cycles that do not improve the Shannon capacity lower bound. The constructions were discovered through iterative interactions with a Large Language Model (LLM), illustrating the potential of LLMs for finding explicit combinatorial constructions.

</details>
